#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""§P-TYPE-WRITE-V2 — coverage-matched weight-write re-test (FROZEN s322).

Pre-reg: mementum/knowledge/explore/types-are-injectable-relations.md §15.

The s322 audit (§14) found §9/§13 COVERAGE-GAPPED: membership-CE gradients
concentrate at the class-word position while licensing is evaluated in a
bare-NP regime the LoRA never gradient-touched — recall-check/licensing-fail
follows even if weight-installable licensing exists. V2 closes the gap while
keeping generalization as the test:

SINGLE FACTOR changed vs §8: TRAINING COVERAGE.
  - training texts = §8's five classificatory statements (verbatim)
    + four bare-NP licensed frames "The {w} {train_pred}." (true class only)
  - TRAIN_PREDS disjoint from §8's HELD_PREDS; eval stays on HELD_PREDS
    verbatim (comparability with §9) + TRAIN_PREDS (the V4 contrast)
  - control fixed: TRUE derangement (1-labels; §14: v1 permutation left
    ~50% labels correct), matched budget (replays wire per-seed stop step)

Everything else verbatim §8/§9-r3: qwen3-4b, FFN band 0.60-0.80, LoRA r=16,
corridor recipe (kl_weight 10 / ce_budget 0.40 via CLI, evidence-gated stop,
fib snaps, REPLAY/CE texts), L(w) = surprisal(anti) - surprisal(own).

Gates (frozen §15):
  V1 HELD-TRANSFER      mean signed L on HELD preds beats label-perm null.
  V2 CLASS-SPECIFIC     own drop > anti drop on HELD preds (paired).
  V3 DERANGED-NULL      wire beats 1-labels wire on HELD L (paired).
  V4 COVERAGE-CONTRAST  TRAIN-pred licensing (label-perm null + deranged
                        paired) — train-lift without held-lift = MEMORIZED-ONLY.
  V5 HOST-SANE (adv.)   real members licensed; host CE in budget; restore.
Verdicts + a-priori (declared, NOT tuned):
  TYPE-WRITTEN 30 / MEMORIZED-ONLY 35 / CONTEXT-ONLY 20 / NO-WRITE 10 /
  HOST-DAMAGED 5.

Harness (lambda one_way, NO fork): imports type_write for the frozen
construction constants, metric, stop rule and recipe; writeback_compile for
LoRALinear; operand_multihop3 for resolve_parts/first_tid.

License: MIT (lambda provenance).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

_HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(_HERE) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HERE))

import type_write as tw  # noqa: E402  (frozen v1 apparatus, verbatim reuse)
from holo_cap import NONCE_CANDS  # noqa: E402

from verbum.dsp.nulls import (  # noqa: E402
    Register,
    gate,
    paired_permutation,
    shuffled_label,
)

# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Construction delta (FROZEN §15). Everything else = tw.* verbatim.
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Class-selective TRAINING predicates — disjoint from tw.HELD_PREDS.
TRAIN_PREDS = (("ate", "drank", "wandered", "rested"),      # animal
               ("braked", "reversed", "idled", "honked"))    # vehicle

assert not (set(TRAIN_PREDS[0]) | set(TRAIN_PREDS[1])) \
    & (set(tw.HELD_PREDS[0]) | set(tw.HELD_PREDS[1])), \
    "TRAIN_PREDS must be disjoint from HELD_PREDS"


def _train_texts(w: str, cls_i: int) -> list[str]:
    """§8 classificatory statements + bare-NP licensed frames (true class).
    The bare-NP forward regime — nonce subject, loss reaching the predicate
    token — is gradient-touched on DIFFERENT predicates than eval."""
    return tw._member_stmts(w, cls_i) + \
        [f"The {w} {p}." for p in TRAIN_PREDS[cls_i]]


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Pure statistics + verdict (what --validate exercises; no torch, no model)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def compute_gates_v2(b: dict, rng: np.random.Generator, alpha: float = 0.05,
                     n_iter: int = 10000) -> dict:
    """b: per-nonce arrays for base/wire/deranged x held/train + recall/host."""
    labels = np.asarray(b["labels"], int)

    def L(tag: str, pool: str) -> np.ndarray:
        return tw._signed_L(b[f"sA_{tag}_{pool}"], b[f"sV_{tag}_{pool}"],
                            labels)

    L_wire_h, L_der_h = L("wire", "held"), L("der", "held")
    L_wire_t, L_der_t = L("wire", "train"), L("der", "train")
    L_base_h, L_base_t = L("base", "held"), L("base", "train")
    recall_w = tw._signed_recall(b["rA_wire"], b["rV_wire"], labels)

    # own/anti surprisal drops on HELD (base - wire), by label
    sA_b, sV_b = np.asarray(b["sA_base_held"], float), np.asarray(b["sV_base_held"], float)  # noqa: E501
    sA_w, sV_w = np.asarray(b["sA_wire_held"], float), np.asarray(b["sV_wire_held"], float)  # noqa: E501
    own_b = np.where(labels == 0, sA_b, sV_b)
    anti_b = np.where(labels == 0, sV_b, sA_b)
    own_w = np.where(labels == 0, sA_w, sV_w)
    anti_w = np.where(labels == 0, sV_w, sA_w)
    d_own, d_anti = own_b - own_w, anti_b - anti_w

    kw = {"claim_register": Register.value, "probe_register": Register.value}

    # ── V1 HELD-TRANSFER (≡ TW1 on held) ──
    def stat_Lh(perm_labels):
        return float(np.mean(tw._signed_L(sA_w, sV_w, perm_labels)))
    v1_null = shuffled_label(stat_Lh, labels, rng, n_iter=min(n_iter, 2000))
    v1 = gate(stat_Lh(labels), v1_null, "greater", alpha,
              "V1_held_transfer", **kw)

    # ── V2 CLASS-SPECIFIC (≡ TW4 on held) ──
    v2_null = paired_permutation(d_own, d_anti, rng, n_iter=n_iter)
    v2 = gate(float(np.mean(d_own - d_anti)), v2_null, "greater", alpha,
              "V2_class_specific", **kw)

    # ── V3 DERANGED-NULL (≡ TW3, control = 1-labels wire) ──
    v3_null = paired_permutation(L_wire_h, L_der_h, rng, n_iter=n_iter)
    v3 = gate(float(np.mean(L_wire_h - L_der_h)), v3_null, "greater", alpha,
              "V3_deranged_null", **kw)

    # ── V4 COVERAGE-CONTRAST: TRAIN-pred licensing (T1 label-perm, T3 paired)
    sA_wt = np.asarray(b["sA_wire_train"], float)
    sV_wt = np.asarray(b["sV_wire_train"], float)

    def stat_Lt(perm_labels):
        return float(np.mean(tw._signed_L(sA_wt, sV_wt, perm_labels)))
    t1_null = shuffled_label(stat_Lt, labels, rng, n_iter=min(n_iter, 2000))
    t1 = gate(stat_Lt(labels), t1_null, "greater", alpha,
              "V4_train_transfer", **kw)
    t3_null = paired_permutation(L_wire_t, L_der_t, rng, n_iter=n_iter)
    t3 = gate(float(np.mean(L_wire_t - L_der_t)), t3_null, "greater", alpha,
              "V4_train_vs_deranged", **kw)

    # ── membership recall (trained frame): NO-WRITE split (≡ v1) ──
    rA_w, rV_w = np.asarray(b["rA_wire"], float), np.asarray(b["rV_wire"], float)

    def stat_recall(perm_labels):
        return float(np.mean(tw._signed_recall(rA_w, rV_w, perm_labels)))
    rec_null = shuffled_label(stat_recall, labels, rng,
                              n_iter=min(n_iter, 2000))
    rec = gate(stat_recall(labels), rec_null, "greater", alpha,
               "membership_recall", **kw)

    # ── V5 HOST-SANE (≡ TW5) ──
    host = b.get("host", {})
    ce_ok = (host.get("ce_wire", 0.0) - host.get("ce_base", 0.0)) <= tw.CE_TOL
    real_ok = host.get("real_L_wire_mean", 1.0) > 0.0
    restore_ok = bool(host.get("restore_ok", True))
    host_sane = bool(ce_ok and real_ok and restore_ok)

    held_ok = bool(v1.verdict and v2.verdict and v3.verdict)
    train_lift = bool(t1.verdict and t3.verdict)
    recall_ok = bool(rec.verdict)

    if not recall_ok:
        verdict = "NO-WRITE"
    elif not host_sane:
        verdict = "HOST-DAMAGED"
    elif held_ok:
        verdict = "TYPE-WRITTEN"
    elif train_lift:
        verdict = "MEMORIZED-ONLY"
    else:
        verdict = "CONTEXT-ONLY"

    return {
        "verdict": verdict,
        "held_ok": held_ok, "train_lift": train_lift,
        "recall_ok": recall_ok, "host_sane": host_sane,
        "gates": {
            "V1": tw._gd(v1), "V2": tw._gd(v2), "V3": tw._gd(v3),
            "V4_train": tw._gd(t1), "V4_train_vs_der": tw._gd(t3),
            "membership_recall": tw._gd(rec),
            "V5_host": {"ce_ok": ce_ok, "real_ok": real_ok,
                        "restore_ok": restore_ok, "pass": host_sane},
        },
        "means": {
            "L_base_held": float(np.mean(L_base_h)),
            "L_wire_held": float(np.mean(L_wire_h)),
            "L_der_held": float(np.mean(L_der_h)),
            "L_base_train": float(np.mean(L_base_t)),
            "L_wire_train": float(np.mean(L_wire_t)),
            "L_der_train": float(np.mean(L_der_t)),
            "recall_wire": float(np.mean(recall_w)),
            "n_nonce": int(labels.size),
        },
    }


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# --validate: planted worlds (no model)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def _world(rng, kind: str, n: int = 24) -> dict:
    labels = np.array([0, 1] * (n // 2))
    host = {"ce_base": 3.0, "ce_wire": 3.05, "real_L_wire_mean": 1.2,
            "restore_ok": True}

    def base_pool():
        return rng.normal(6.0, 0.4, n), rng.normal(6.0, 0.4, n)

    def drop_own(sA, sV, amount):
        sA, sV = sA.copy(), sV.copy()
        for i in range(n):
            (sA, sV)[labels[i]][i] -= amount[i]
        return sA, sV

    def drop_anti(sA, sV, amount):
        sA, sV = sA.copy(), sV.copy()
        for i in range(n):
            (sA, sV)[1 - labels[i]][i] -= amount[i]
        return sA, sV

    sA_bh, sV_bh = base_pool()
    sA_bt, sV_bt = base_pool()
    big = rng.uniform(1.2, 2.2, n)
    noise = rng.normal(0, 0.1, n)

    # recall installs in every world except no_write
    rA_w = rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, n)
    rV_w = rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, n)
    if kind != "no_write":
        for i in range(n):
            (rA_w, rV_w)[labels[i]][i] += rng.uniform(2.0, 3.0)

    if kind == "type_written":       # held AND train lift; deranged lifts anti
        sA_wh, sV_wh = drop_own(sA_bh, sV_bh, big)
        sA_wt, sV_wt = drop_own(sA_bt, sV_bt, big)
        sA_dh, sV_dh = drop_anti(sA_bh, sV_bh, big)
        sA_dt, sV_dt = drop_anti(sA_bt, sV_bt, big)
    elif kind == "memorized_only":   # train lifts, held does NOT
        sA_wh, sV_wh = sA_bh + noise, sV_bh + noise
        sA_wt, sV_wt = drop_own(sA_bt, sV_bt, big)
        sA_dh, sV_dh = sA_bh + noise, sV_bh + noise
        sA_dt, sV_dt = drop_anti(sA_bt, sV_bt, big)
    elif kind in ("context_only", "no_write"):   # nothing licenses
        sA_wh, sV_wh = sA_bh + noise, sV_bh + noise
        sA_wt, sV_wt = sA_bt + noise, sV_bt + noise
        sA_dh, sV_dh = sA_bh + noise, sV_bh + noise
        sA_dt, sV_dt = sA_bt + noise, sV_bt + noise
    elif kind == "host_damaged":     # transfer present but host burned
        sA_wh, sV_wh = drop_own(sA_bh, sV_bh, big)
        sA_wt, sV_wt = drop_own(sA_bt, sV_bt, big)
        sA_dh, sV_dh = sA_bh + noise, sV_bh + noise
        sA_dt, sV_dt = sA_bt + noise, sV_bt + noise
        host = {"ce_base": 3.0, "ce_wire": 9.0, "real_L_wire_mean": -0.5,
                "restore_ok": False}
    else:
        raise ValueError(kind)

    return {"labels": labels,
            "sA_base_held": sA_bh, "sV_base_held": sV_bh,
            "sA_base_train": sA_bt, "sV_base_train": sV_bt,
            "sA_wire_held": sA_wh, "sV_wire_held": sV_wh,
            "sA_wire_train": sA_wt, "sV_wire_train": sV_wt,
            "sA_der_held": sA_dh, "sV_der_held": sV_dh,
            "sA_der_train": sA_dt, "sV_der_train": sV_dt,
            "rA_wire": rA_w, "rV_wire": rV_w, "host": host}


def run_validate(alpha: float) -> int:
    print("── §P-TYPE-WRITE-V2 --validate (planted worlds, no model) ──")
    want = {"type_written": "TYPE-WRITTEN",
            "memorized_only": "MEMORIZED-ONLY",
            "context_only": "CONTEXT-ONLY",
            "no_write": "NO-WRITE",
            "host_damaged": "HOST-DAMAGED"}
    ok = True
    for kind, expect in want.items():
        rng = np.random.default_rng(hash(kind) % (2**31))
        b = _world(rng, kind)
        res = compute_gates_v2(b, rng, alpha, n_iter=2000)
        got = res["verdict"]
        good = got == expect
        ok &= good
        print(f"  {kind:16s} -> {got:16s} expect {expect:16s} "
              f"{'✓' if good else '✗ FAIL'}")
    # primitives
    lab = np.array([0, 1, 0, 1])
    der = 1 - lab
    prim = bool(np.all(der != lab))
    ok &= prim
    print(f"  primitive 1-labels true derangement  {'✓' if prim else '✗ FAIL'}")
    disjoint = not (set(TRAIN_PREDS[0]) | set(TRAIN_PREDS[1])) \
        & (set(tw.HELD_PREDS[0]) | set(tw.HELD_PREDS[1]))
    ok &= disjoint
    print(f"  primitive TRAIN∩HELD = ∅             {'✓' if disjoint else '✗ FAIL'}")
    texts = _train_texts("wug", 0)
    cover = (len(texts) == 9 and sum(t.startswith("The wug ") for t in texts) >= 4
             and all(p not in " ".join(texts)
                     for p in tw.HELD_PREDS[0] + tw.HELD_PREDS[1]))
    ok &= cover
    print(f"  primitive train texts: 5 stmts + 4 bare-NP, no held preds  "
          f"{'✓' if cover else '✗ FAIL'}")
    print(f"\n── --validate {'ALL PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} ──")
    return 0 if ok else 1


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Model path (structure = v1 run_model; deltas: train texts, dual-pool eval,
# true derangement)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
def run_model(args) -> int:
    import operand_multihop3 as mh3
    import torch
    import torch.nn.functional as F
    import writeback_compile as wb
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

    dev = (args.device if (args.device != "mps"
                           or torch.backends.mps.is_available()) else "cpu")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(args.seed)
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_id)
    if tok.pad_token is None:
        tok.pad_token = tok.eos_token
    tok.padding_side = "right"
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        args.model_id, dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype)).to(dev).eval()
    for p in model.parameters():
        p.requires_grad_(False)
    dec, _norm, _lm_head = mh3.resolve_parts(model)
    n_layers = len(dec)
    band = list(range(round(tw.BAND_FRAC[0] * n_layers),
                      round(tw.BAND_FRAC[1] * n_layers) + 1))
    out_dir = Path(args.out)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    print(f"[tw2] {args.model_id} dev={dev} n_layers={n_layers} "
          f"band=L{band[0]}..L{band[-1]} seeds={args.seeds} steps={args.steps}")

    def tid(w: str) -> int:
        return mh3.first_tid(tok, w)

    def logp_last(prompt: str) -> np.ndarray:
        ids = tok(prompt, return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
        with torch.no_grad():
            lo = model(**ids).logits[0, -1, :].float()
        return F.log_softmax(lo, dim=-1).cpu().numpy()

    def surprisal(prefix: str, cont: str) -> float:
        pre = tok(prefix, return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
        full = tok(prefix + cont, return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
        n_pre = pre.input_ids.shape[1]
        with torch.no_grad():
            lo = model(**full).logits[0].float()
        lp = F.log_softmax(lo, dim=-1)
        tgt = full.input_ids[0]
        s = 0.0
        for pos in range(n_pre, tgt.shape[0]):
            s += float(lp[pos - 1, tgt[pos]])
        return -s

    def ce_host() -> float:
        tot, n = 0.0, 0
        for t in tw.CE_TEXTS:
            ids = tok(t, return_tensors="pt").to(dev)
            with torch.no_grad():
                lo = model(**ids).logits[0].float()
            lp = F.log_softmax(lo[:-1], dim=-1)
            tgt = ids.input_ids[0, 1:]
            tot += float(-lp[torch.arange(len(tgt)), tgt].sum())
            n += len(tgt)
        return tot / max(n, 1)

    def eval_pool(members: list[str], preds) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        sA, sV = [], []
        for w in members:
            frame = f"The {w}"
            sA.append(np.mean([surprisal(frame, " " + p) for p in preds[0]]))
            sV.append(np.mean([surprisal(frame, " " + p) for p in preds[1]]))
        return np.array(sA), np.array(sV)

    def eval_members(members: list[str]) -> dict:
        aA_tid, aV_tid = tid("animal"), tid("vehicle")
        sA_h, sV_h = eval_pool(members, tw.HELD_PREDS)
        sA_t, sV_t = eval_pool(members, TRAIN_PREDS)
        rA, rV = [], []
        for w in members:
            lp = logp_last(f"A {w} is a kind of")
            rA.append(float(lp[aA_tid]))
            rV.append(float(lp[aV_tid]))
        return {"sA_h": sA_h, "sV_h": sV_h, "sA_t": sA_t, "sV_t": sV_t,
                "rA": np.array(rA), "rV": np.array(rV)}

    # ── nonce usability + class assignment (≡ v1) ──
    nonces, labels = [], []
    for i, w in enumerate(NONCE_CANDS):
        n_the = tok("The", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
        n_thew = tok(f"The {w}", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
        if len(n_thew) - len(n_the) >= 1:
            nonces.append(w)
            labels.append(i % 2)
    if args.n_nonce:
        keep = args.n_nonce
        a = [j for j, in_ in enumerate(labels) if in_ == 0][:keep // 2]
        v = [j for j, in_ in enumerate(labels) if in_ == 1][:keep // 2]
        sel = sorted(a + v)
        nonces = [nonces[j] for j in sel]
        labels = [labels[j] for j in sel]
    labels = np.array(labels, int)
    n = len(nonces)
    print(f"[tw2] nonces={n} (animal {int((labels == 0).sum())} "
          f"vehicle {int((labels == 1).sum())})")

    # ── gate-0: base real-member licensing on BOTH pred pools ──
    print("[tw2] gate-0: base licensing of real members (held + train) …")
    real_members = list(tw.REAL_MEMBERS[0]) + list(tw.REAL_MEMBERS[1])
    real_labels = np.array([0] * len(tw.REAL_MEMBERS[0])
                           + [1] * len(tw.REAL_MEMBERS[1]))
    rb_e = eval_members(real_members)
    L_real_h = tw._signed_L(rb_e["sA_h"], rb_e["sV_h"], real_labels)
    L_real_t = tw._signed_L(rb_e["sA_t"], rb_e["sV_t"], real_labels)
    m_h, m_t = float(np.mean(L_real_h)), float(np.mean(L_real_t))
    per_class_ok = all(
        np.mean(Lr[real_labels == c]) > 0
        for Lr in (L_real_h, L_real_t) for c in (0, 1))
    n_ok = (labels == 0).sum() >= args.min_class and \
           (labels == 1).sum() >= args.min_class
    gate0_ok = bool(m_h >= tw.REAL_MARGIN_FLOOR
                    and m_t >= tw.REAL_MARGIN_FLOOR
                    and per_class_ok and n_ok)
    print(f"[tw2] gate-0: real margin held={m_h:.3f} train={m_t:.3f} "
          f"per_class_ok={per_class_ok} n_ok={n_ok} "
          f"-> {'PASS' if gate0_ok else 'FAIL'}")
    (out_dir / "gate0.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        "model_id": args.model_id, "n_nonce": n,
        "real_margin_held": m_h, "real_margin_train": m_t,
        "per_class_ok": bool(per_class_ok), "gate0_ok": gate0_ok,
        "nonces": nonces, "labels": labels.tolist()}, indent=2))
    if args.gate0_only:
        return 0 if gate0_ok else 1
    if not gate0_ok and not args.force:
        print("[tw2] gate-0 FAIL — stopping (use --force to override)")
        return 1

    # ── base arm ──
    print("[tw2] arm base …")
    base = eval_members(nonces)
    ce_base = ce_host()

    # ── replay anchor cache (≡ v1 s315 amendment) ──
    rb = tok(tw.REPLAY_TEXTS, return_tensors="pt", padding=True).to(dev)
    with torch.no_grad():
        base_lo = model(**rb).logits.float()
        p_base_replay = torch.softmax(base_lo, dim=-1)
        h_base_replay = -(p_base_replay
                          * F.log_softmax(base_lo, dim=-1)).sum(-1)
    replay_mask = rb.attention_mask.float()
    del base_lo
    print(f"[tw2] replay anchor cached: {len(tw.REPLAY_TEXTS)} texts, "
          f"{int(replay_mask.sum())} positions, kl_weight={args.kl_weight}")

    # ── wire trainer (≡ v1 except stmts = _train_texts) ──
    def train_wire(train_labels: np.ndarray, seed: int,
                   stop_at: int | None = None):
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        wrapped, params = [], []
        for li in band:
            m = dec[li].mlp
            for name in ("gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"):
                orig = getattr(m, name)
                lw = wb.LoRALinear(orig, r=args.lora_r, alpha=2 * args.lora_r)
                setattr(m, name, lw)
                wrapped.append((m, name, orig))
                params += [lw.A, lw.B]
        opt = torch.optim.Adam(params, lr=args.lr)
        stmts = [s for w, lb in zip(nonces, train_labels, strict=True)
                 for s in _train_texts(w, int(lb))]
        batch = tok(stmts, return_tensors="pt", padding=True).to(dev)
        ids, attn = batch.input_ids, batch.attention_mask
        snap_set = {s for s in tw.FIB_SNAPS if s < args.steps}
        hist: dict = {"step": [], "mem_ce": [], "kl": [],
                      "host_ce": [], "drift": []}
        n_steps = args.steps if stop_at is None else stop_at
        stop_step, stop_reason = n_steps, ("max_steps" if stop_at is None
                                           else "matched_budget")
        last_good = [p.detach().clone() for p in params]
        last_good_step = -1
        for step in range(n_steps):
            opt.zero_grad()
            lo = model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=attn).logits.float()
            shift_lo = lo[:, :-1, :]
            shift_tg = ids[:, 1:]
            shift_m = attn[:, 1:].float()
            ce = F.cross_entropy(
                shift_lo.reshape(-1, shift_lo.shape[-1]),
                shift_tg.reshape(-1), reduction="none").reshape(shift_tg.shape)
            mem_ce = (ce * shift_m).sum() / shift_m.sum().clamp_min(1.0)
            lo_r = model(**rb).logits.float()
            lq = F.log_softmax(lo_r, dim=-1)
            kl = ((-(p_base_replay * lq).sum(-1) - h_base_replay)
                  * replay_mask).sum() / replay_mask.sum()
            loss = mem_ce + args.kl_weight * kl
            loss.backward()
            opt.step()
            if step in snap_set:
                ce_h = ce_host()
                hist["step"].append(step)
                hist["mem_ce"].append(float(mem_ce.detach()))
                hist["kl"].append(float(kl.detach()))
                hist["host_ce"].append(ce_h)
                hist["drift"].append(ce_h - ce_base)
                print(f"    seed{seed} snap {step:4d} mem "
                      f"{hist['mem_ce'][-1]:.4f} kl {hist['kl'][-1]:.4f} "
                      f"host_ce {ce_h:.4f} drift {hist['drift'][-1]:+.4f}",
                      flush=True)
                if stop_at is None:
                    keep, reason = tw._stop_decision(
                        hist["step"], hist["mem_ce"], hist["drift"],
                        args.ce_budget, args.plateau_tol, args.min_stop)
                    if reason == "plateau":
                        stop_step, stop_reason = keep, reason
                        print(f"    seed{seed} STOP plateau @ step {step} "
                              f"(keep {keep})", flush=True)
                        break
                    if reason == "ce_budget_rollback":
                        with torch.no_grad():
                            for p, g in zip(params, last_good, strict=True):
                                p.copy_(g)
                        stop_step, stop_reason = keep, reason
                        print(f"    seed{seed} STOP ce-budget @ step {step} "
                              f"-> rollback to step {last_good_step} "
                              f"(keep {keep})", flush=True)
                        break
                    last_good = [p.detach().clone() for p in params]
                    last_good_step = step

        def unwrap():
            for m, name, orig in wrapped:
                setattr(m, name, orig)
        info = {"stop_step": int(stop_step), "stop_reason": stop_reason,
                "seed": seed, "history": hist}
        return unwrap, info

    def accum(train_labels, tag, stops=None):
        acc = {k: [] for k in ("sA_h", "sV_h", "sA_t", "sV_t", "rA", "rV")}
        real_L, ce_w, infos = [], [], []
        for sd in range(args.seeds):
            unwrap, info = train_wire(
                train_labels, sd,
                stop_at=None if stops is None else stops[sd])
            infos.append(info)
            e = eval_members(nonces)      # eval ALWAYS on the same frames
            for k in acc:
                acc[k].append(e[k])
            if sd == 0:
                rme = eval_members(real_members)
                real_L.append(float(np.mean(tw._signed_L(
                    rme["sA_h"], rme["sV_h"], real_labels))))
                ce_w.append(ce_host())
            unwrap()
            print(f"[tw2] {tag} seed{sd} done "
                  f"(stop {info['stop_step']} {info['stop_reason']})",
                  flush=True)
        return ({k: np.mean(acc[k], axis=0) for k in acc},
                (real_L[0] if real_L else np.nan),
                (ce_w[0] if ce_w else np.nan), infos)

    print("[tw2] arm wire (true membership + bare-NP coverage) …")
    wire, real_L_wire, ce_wire, wire_infos = accum(labels, "wire")
    wire_stops = [i["stop_step"] for i in wire_infos]

    # TRUE derangement (§15: every label flipped; matched budget)
    der_labels = 1 - labels
    assert np.all(der_labels != labels)
    print(f"[tw2] arm deranged (1-labels, matched budget {wire_stops}) …")
    der, _, _, der_infos = accum(der_labels, "deranged", stops=wire_stops)

    # ── restore check ──
    base2 = eval_members(nonces[:2])
    restore_ok = bool(np.allclose(base2["sA_h"], base["sA_h"][:2], atol=1e-3))

    bundle = {
        "labels": labels,
        "sA_base_held": base["sA_h"], "sV_base_held": base["sV_h"],
        "sA_base_train": base["sA_t"], "sV_base_train": base["sV_t"],
        "sA_wire_held": wire["sA_h"], "sV_wire_held": wire["sV_h"],
        "sA_wire_train": wire["sA_t"], "sV_wire_train": wire["sV_t"],
        "sA_der_held": der["sA_h"], "sV_der_held": der["sV_h"],
        "sA_der_train": der["sA_t"], "sV_der_train": der["sV_t"],
        "rA_wire": wire["rA"], "rV_wire": wire["rV"],
        "host": {"ce_base": ce_base, "ce_wire": ce_wire,
                 "real_L_wire_mean": real_L_wire, "restore_ok": restore_ok},
    }
    res = compute_gates_v2(bundle, rng, args.alpha)
    res["meta"] = {
        "model_id": args.model_id, "n_nonce": n, "seeds": args.seeds,
        "steps": args.steps, "lr": args.lr, "lora_r": args.lora_r,
        "band": [band[0], band[-1]], "gate0_ok": gate0_ok,
        "nonces": nonces, "labels": labels.tolist(),
        "real_margin_held": m_h, "real_margin_train": m_t,
        "ce_base": ce_base, "ce_wire": ce_wire,
        "real_L_wire": real_L_wire, "restore_ok": restore_ok,
        "kl_weight": args.kl_weight, "ce_budget": args.ce_budget,
        "plateau_tol": args.plateau_tol, "min_stop": args.min_stop,
        "train_preds": [list(TRAIN_PREDS[0]), list(TRAIN_PREDS[1])],
        "held_preds": [list(tw.HELD_PREDS[0]), list(tw.HELD_PREDS[1])],
        "wire_stops": wire_stops,
        "wire_stop_reasons": [i["stop_reason"] for i in wire_infos],
    }
    res["training"] = {"wire": wire_infos, "deranged": der_infos}
    res["per_nonce"] = {
        "L_wire_held": tw._signed_L(wire["sA_h"], wire["sV_h"], labels).tolist(),
        "L_base_held": tw._signed_L(base["sA_h"], base["sV_h"], labels).tolist(),
        "L_der_held": tw._signed_L(der["sA_h"], der["sV_h"], labels).tolist(),
        "L_wire_train": tw._signed_L(wire["sA_t"], wire["sV_t"], labels).tolist(),
        "L_base_train": tw._signed_L(base["sA_t"], base["sV_t"], labels).tolist(),
        "L_der_train": tw._signed_L(der["sA_t"], der["sV_t"], labels).tolist(),
        "recall_wire": tw._signed_recall(wire["rA"], wire["rV"], labels).tolist(),
    }
    (out_dir / "results.json").write_text(json.dumps(res, indent=2))
    print(f"[tw2] wrote {out_dir}/results.json")
    g = res["gates"]
    print(f"[tw2] V1 p={g['V1']['p']:.4f} pass={g['V1']['pass']} | "
          f"V2 p={g['V2']['p']:.4f} pass={g['V2']['pass']} | "
          f"V3 p={g['V3']['p']:.4f} pass={g['V3']['pass']} | "
          f"V4t p={g['V4_train']['p']:.4f} pass={g['V4_train']['pass']} | "
          f"V4d p={g['V4_train_vs_der']['p']:.4f} "
          f"pass={g['V4_train_vs_der']['pass']} | "
          f"recall pass={g['membership_recall']['pass']} | "
          f"host={res['host_sane']}")
    m = res["means"]
    print(f"[tw2] held: base={m['L_base_held']:.3f} wire={m['L_wire_held']:.3f} "
          f"der={m['L_der_held']:.3f} | train: base={m['L_base_train']:.3f} "
          f"wire={m['L_wire_train']:.3f} der={m['L_der_train']:.3f}")
    print(f"[tw2] VERDICT: {res['verdict']}")
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("--validate", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--gate0-only", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--model-id", default="Qwen/Qwen3-4B")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="mps")
    ap.add_argument("--dtype", default="bfloat16",
                    choices=["float32", "bfloat16"])
    ap.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=500)
    ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-4)
    ap.add_argument("--lora-r", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--min-class", type=int, default=8)
    ap.add_argument("--kl-weight", type=float, default=10.0,
                    help="s315 corridor: KL(base||wire) replay anchor (r3)")
    ap.add_argument("--ce-budget", type=float, default=0.40,
                    help="s315 corridor: max host-CE drift before rollback (r3)")
    ap.add_argument("--plateau-tol", type=float, default=0.01)
    ap.add_argument("--min-stop", type=int, default=55)
    ap.add_argument("--alpha", type=float, default=0.05)
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--n-nonce", type=int, default=0,
                    help="smoke: cap nonces (balanced); 0=all")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="results/type-write-v2/qwen3-4b")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    if args.validate:
        return run_validate(args.alpha)
    return run_model(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

### Thinking

Now I'm seeing the full implementation details from the type_write_v2.py file. The LoRA wrapping applies low-rank adapters with specific rank and alpha values to the MLP projection layers within each band. The training loop uses an Adam optimizer with a combined loss of cross-entropy and KL divergence weighted together, running on replay texts with Fibonacci-spaced snapshots and an evidence-gated stopping mechanism that can trigger rollbacks.

For the flip_conflict variant, I need to fork the training loop significantly because I have to track per-snapshot per-coordinate gradient information separately for each class. This means computing gradients of the class-A-only loss and class-B-only loss independently with respect to the effective weight updates (either directly or through the LoRA A and B matrices), then capturing the sign and magnitude of those updates at each snapshot point.

Now I'm looking at how the LoRA structure maps to the actual weight deltas—specifically how B@A produces the gate_proj weight changes, and I need to understand the dimensions involved (the output and input sizes of the gate projection layer) to properly track which entries fall within the target band. I need to hook into the layer's forward and backward passes to capture the input and output gradients, then compute the outer product to get dL/dΔW directly. This is more complex than working purely in LoRA space, but it gives me the actual effective weight gradient needed for the conflict measure. Now I need to set up the capture infrastructure.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""§P-WRITEBACK-1 rung 3b — BACKPROP-COMPILE: internalize the pin.

Pre-reg: mementum/knowledge/explore/program-plates-and-the-function-index.md
§P-WRITEBACK-1 (FROZEN s302, Michael-approved). The splice-exhaustion table
(s295) fixed the target by elimination: the 0.20→0.90 gap is the WRITEBACK —
only the generation path can produce, commit, and re-encode the hop-2
intermediate. This instrument tests whether that capability can be compiled
into a small persistent WEIGHT delta, as a WIRE (generalizes to held-out
landmarks AND held-out countries) and not a LOOKUP (materialized g∘h view).

Chain (shortcut-free): landmark --g(country-of)--> country
--h(capital-of)--> capital, landmark's own city != capital.

Splits: TRAIN (8 countries x 2 landmarks) / B1 held-landmark (new landmarks
of TRAIN countries) / B2 held-COUNTRY (all landmarks of 8 never-trained
countries — the sharp wire gate).

Arms (all evaluated on the ONE-SHOT direct prompt, greedy first-token argmax
over the union candidate set; margins + generations advisory):
  base             : untouched host (floor).
  construct        : zero-gradient — appended FFN neurons at the install
                     layer: key = whitened country-class filter (shared-Sigma,
                     prompt-shaped innocents law), value = capital unembed
                     direction, gain closed-loop calibrated on COUNTRY frames
                     (never sees a landmark->capital pair).
  construct_shuffle: same keys, deranged capital values (specificity null).
  construct_lookup : landmark-keyed neurons writing the capital directly,
                     TRAIN pairs only (the materialized-view null — must
                     fail B2 by construction).
  gd_cd            : backprop-compile proper — LoRA r=16 FFN-only on the
                     0.6-0.8 band; teacher = SAME host on its own committed
                     CoT ("The {lm} is located in {c}. The capital of {c}
                     is"), student = one-shot prompt; KL at answer position.
  gd_sft           : matched-budget direct answer CE (no tape).
  gd_shuffle       : gd_cd with deranged countries in the teacher CoT.

Gates (verbum.dsp, paired permutation 10k; primaries G1-G3 Bonferroni
alpha/3; G1-G3 routing register, G4/G5 value register):
  G1 WIRE       : arm > base acc with flip on B1 AND B2.
  G2 NOT-LOOKUP : arm > construct_lookup acc on B2.
  G3 SPECIFICITY: arm > its shuffle null on held-out (B1 + B2).
  G4 PIN        : whitened country readout at install layer rises on
                  held-out one-shot prompts + separates correct/incorrect
                  (mechanism clause; reported, never gates alone).
  G5 SURVIVE    : innocent-text CE within 2% rel of base; native g/h accs
                  within 0.10 absolute.
Verdicts: WIRE-COMPILES(+CONSTRUCTION-SUFFICES/+GD-REQUIRED/+BOTH) /
LOOKUP-ONLY / UNSPECIFIC / HOST-DAMAGED / STILL-EXTERNAL.

    class LoRALinear(_torch.nn.Module):
        def __init__(self, base: _torch.nn.Module, r: int, alpha: float):
            super().__init__()
            self.base = base
            for p in self.base.parameters():
                p.requires_grad_(False)
            din = base.in_features
            dout = base.out_features
            dev = base.weight.device
            self.A = _torch.nn.Parameter(
                _torch.randn(r, din, device=dev, dtype=_torch.float32) * 0.01)
            self.B = _torch.nn.Parameter(
                _torch.zeros(dout, r, device=dev, dtype=_torch.float32))
            self.scale = alpha / r

        def forward(self, x):
            y = self.base(x)
            lo = (x.to(self.A.dtype) @ self.A.T) @ self.B.T
            return y + (self.scale * lo).to(y.dtype)
except Exception:                                     # pragma: no cover
    LoRALinear = None  # type: ignore[assignment]

def _signed_L(sA: np.ndarray, sV: np.ndarray, labels: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Per-nonce L = surprisal(anti) - surprisal(own), sign fixed by label.
    label 0 (animal): own=sA anti=sV -> L=sV-sA ; label 1: L=sA-sV."""
    sA, sV = np.asarray(sA, float), np.asarray(sV, float)
    lab = np.asarray(labels, int)
    own = np.where(lab == 0, sA, sV)
    anti = np.where(lab == 0, sV, sA)
    return anti - own


def _signed_recall(rA: np.ndarray, rV: np.ndarray,
                   labels: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Membership-recall margin = logp(own class token) - logp(anti)."""
    rA, rV = np.asarray(rA, float), np.asarray(rV, float)
    lab = np.asarray(labels, int)
    own = np.where(lab == 0, rA, rV)
    anti = np.where(lab == 0, rV, rA)
    return own - anti


def _spearman(x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> float:
    x, y = np.asarray(x, float), np.asarray(y, float)
    if x.size < 3:
        return 0.0
    rx = np.argsort(np.argsort(x)).astype(float)
    ry = np.argsort(np.argsort(y)).astype(float)
    rx -= rx.mean()
    ry -= ry.mean()
    denom = np.sqrt((rx @ rx) * (ry @ ry))
    return float(rx @ ry / denom) if denom > 0 else 0.0


def _stop_decision(steps_hist: list, mem_hist: list, drift_hist: list,
                   budget: float, tol: float, min_stop: int) -> tuple:
    """Evidence-gated stop (s315 amendment). Pure; validate-tested.

    Scans per-snap history in order; first firing rule wins. Returns
    (n_steps_to_keep, reason):
      ce_budget_rollback — host-CE drift exceeded budget at a snap; keep only
                           steps up to the PREVIOUS (good) snap.
      plateau            — membership CE rel-improvement between consecutive
                           snaps < tol at a snap >= min_stop; keep current.
      max_steps          — no rule fired; keep everything.
    Used incrementally in-loop (called on the growing history each snap) and
    wholesale in --validate on planted curves — same code path (λ one_way)."""
    prev_mem = None
    for i, (s, m, d) in enumerate(zip(steps_hist, mem_hist, drift_hist,
                                      strict=True)):
        if d > budget:
            keep = 0 if i == 0 else steps_hist[i - 1] + 1
            return keep, "ce_budget_rollback"
        if (prev_mem is not None and s >= min_stop
                and (prev_mem - m) / max(prev_mem, 1e-9) < tol):
            return s + 1, "plateau"
        prev_mem = m
    return (steps_hist[-1] + 1 if steps_hist else 0), "max_steps"


def compute_gates(b: dict, rng: np.random.Generator, alpha: float = 0.05,
                  n_iter: int = 10000) -> dict:
    """b holds per-nonce arm arrays. Returns gates + verdict. Pure."""

    sA_w, sV_w = np.asarray(b["sA_wire"], float), np.asarray(b["sV_wire"], float)

magnitude-weighted class-gradient sign-disagreement,\n                mean_t[ -sign(g_A,k * g_B,k) ], g = dL/ddW via forward/backward\n                hooks (dW enters linearly -> dL/ddW_ij = sum_t gout_i * x_j).\n\nGates (offline, pure; --validate exercises them):\n  G0 SANE/VOID      wire trains + nonzero flip population + captures finite.\n  G1 CONFLICT-METER partial corr(flip, conflict | |W|, sigma) > 0 vs coord-perm\n                    null (primary; confound handled AT the gate).\n  G2 CAUSAL-FREEZE  contested coords freeze (flip drops both->single-pop) more\n                    than matched-|W| low-conflict controls (make-or-break).\n  G3 COMMITTED-POLE high-|W_base| committed coords: low flip AND low conflict.\n  G4 MECHANISM      advisory, non-gating (lambda yardstick): lam_max trajectory\n                    vs 2/eta (edge-of-stability) + SGD-vs-Adam -> EOS /\n                    SIGMA-DELTA / SGD-DITHER / AMBIGUOUS.\nVerdicts + a-priori (declared, NOT tuned): CONFLICT-METER-CONFIRMED 35 /\nCORRELATIONAL-ONLY 30 / NOISE-FLOOR 25 / VOID 10; mechanism sub SIGMA-DELTA 30 /\nEOS 25 / SGD-DITHER 20 / AMBIGUOUS 25.\n\nRun matrix (frozen): both/A-only/B-only x SGD + both x Adam, 3 seeds = 12 runs;\nrich per-snap capture; widened IOU capture (per-class loss + band activation\nmeans, grad-magnitude, |W_base| map, Adam m/v, top-3 Hessian eigs) persisted but\nNOT gated — any claim gets its own null + IOU.\n\nHarness (lambda one_way): imports type_write for the frozen construction,\ncorridor recipe, stop rule; writeback_compile for LoRALinear; operand_multihop3\nfor resolve_parts/first_tid.\n\nLicense: MIT (lambda provenance).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n_HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\nif str(_HERE) not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(_HERE))\n\nimport type_write as tw  # noqa: E402  (frozen corridor + construction, verbatim)\nfrom holo_cap import NONCE_CANDS  # noqa: E402\n\n# Strata for |W_base| stratified sampling of effective-dW coordinates.\nSTRATA = (\"marginal\", \"mid\", \"committed\")\nN_PER_STRATUM = 2000          # per band layer per stratum (48k coords @ 8 layers)\nCONTESTED_Q = 0.75            # G2 contested = top-quartile conflict\nCONTROL_Q = 0.25             # G2 control  = bottom-quartile conflict\nCOMMIT_Q = 0.75              # G3 committed = top-quartile |W_base|\nMARGIN_Q = 0.25             # G3 marginal  = bottom-quartile |W_base|\nFLIP_POP_FLOOR = 0.02        # G0: >= this fraction of coords must ever flip\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# Pure statistics (no torch, no model) — what --validate exercises\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\ndef _rank(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    a = np.asarray(a, float)\n    return np.argsort(np.argsort(a)).astype(float)\n\n\ndef _residualize(y: np.ndarray, C: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Residual of y after least-squares regression on design [1 | C].\"\"\"\n    A = np.column_stack([np.ones(len(y)), C])\n    coef, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(A, y, rcond=None)\n    return y - A @ coef\n\n\ndef _corr(x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> float:\n    x = x - x.mean()\n    y = y - y.mean()\n    d = np.sqrt((x @ x) * (y @ y))\n    return float(x @ y / d) if d > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef partial_spearman(x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray,\n                     ctrls: list[np.ndarray]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Spearman partial correlation of x,y controlling for ctrls (rank-based).\"\"\"\n    rx, ry = _rank(x), _rank(y)\n    C = np.column_stack([_rank(c) for c in ctrls])\n    return _corr(_residualize(rx, C), _residualize(ry, C))\n\n\ndef _pval_ge(obs: float, null: np.ndarray) -> float:\n    null = np.asarray(null, float)\n    return float((np.sum(null >= obs) + 1) / (null.size + 1))\n\n\ndef compute_gates(caps: dict, meta: dict, rng: np.random.Generator,\n                  alpha: float = 0.05, n_perm: int = 2000) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Offline gates from per-arm per-coordinate captures. Pure.\n\n    caps[arm] = {flip, conflict, W_abs, sigma, W_base_abs, lam_max(list)}\n    where arm in {both_sgd, A_sgd, B_sgd, both_adam}. Coordinates aligned\n    across arms (same stratified sample).\"\"\"\n    both = caps[\"both_sgd\"]\n    flip = np.asarray(both[\"flip\"], float)\n    conflict = np.asarray(both[\"conflict\"], float)\n    W_abs = np.asarray(both[\"W_abs\"], float)\n    sigma = np.asarray(both[\"sigma\"], float)\n    W_base_abs = np.asarray(both[\"W_base_abs\"], float)\n    n = flip.size\n\n    finite = all(np.all(np.isfinite(np.asarray(both[k], float)))\n                 for k in (\"flip\", \"conflict\", \"W_abs\", \"sigma\"))\n    flip_pop = float(np.mean(flip > 0))\n    trained = bool(meta.get(\"trained\", True))\n    g0_pass = bool(finite and flip_pop >= FLIP_POP_FLOOR and trained and n >= 100)\n\n    # ── G1 CONFLICT-METER (partial corr | |W|, sigma; coord-perm null) ──\n    obs1 = partial_spearman(flip, conflict, [W_abs, sigma])\n    null1 = np.array([partial_spearman(flip, rng.permutation(conflict),\n                                       [W_abs, sigma]) for _ in range(n_perm)])\n    p1 = _pval_ge(obs1, null1)\n    g1_pass = bool(g0_pass and obs1 > 0 and p1 < alpha)\n\n    # ── G2 CAUSAL-FREEZE (contested vs matched-|W| control; ablation) ──\n    flip_single = 0.5 * (np.asarray(caps[\"A_sgd\"][\"flip\"], float)\n                         + np.asarray(caps[\"B_sgd\"][\"flip\"], float))\n    delta = flip - flip_single                     # >0 = froze when pop removed\n    c_hi = conflict >= np.quantile(conflict, CONTESTED_Q)\n    c_lo = conflict <= np.quantile(conflict, CONTROL_Q)\n    # match control on |W| range of contested\n    wlo, whi = np.quantile(W_abs[c_hi], 0.05), np.quantile(W_abs[c_hi], 0.95)\n    ctrl = c_lo & (W_abs >= wlo) & (W_abs <= whi)\n    if ctrl.sum() < 20:\n        ctrl = c_lo\n    d_con, d_ctrl = delta[c_hi], delta[ctrl]\n    obs2 = float(d_con.mean() - d_ctrl.mean())\n    pooled = np.concatenate([d_con, d_ctrl])\n    n_con = d_con.size\n    null2 = np.empty(n_perm)\n    for i in range(n_perm):\n        pp = rng.permutation(pooled)\n        null2[i] = pp[:n_con].mean() - pp[n_con:].mean()\n    p2 = _pval_ge(obs2, null2)\n    g2_pass = bool(g1_pass and float(d_con.mean()) > 0 and obs2 > 0\n                   and p2 < alpha)\n\n    # ── G3 COMMITTED-POLE (negative control corner) ──\n    commit = W_base_abs >= np.quantile(W_base_abs, COMMIT_Q)\n    margin = W_base_abs <= np.quantile(W_base_abs, MARGIN_Q)\n    g3_flip_ok = bool(flip[commit].mean() < flip[margin].mean())\n    g3_conf_ok = bool(conflict[commit].mean() < conflict[margin].mean())\n    g3_pass = bool(g3_flip_ok and g3_conf_ok)\n\n    # ── G4 MECHANISM-SPLIT (advisory, non-gating) ──\n    mech = _mechanism(caps, meta)\n\n    if not g0_pass:\n        verdict = \"VOID\"\n    elif g1_pass and g2_pass:\n        verdict = \"CONFLICT-METER-CONFIRMED\"\n    elif g1_pass:\n        verdict = \"CORRELATIONAL-ONLY\"\n    else:\n        verdict = \"NOISE-FLOOR\"\n\n    return {\n        \"verdict\": verdict,\n        \"mechanism\": mech[\"label\"],\n        \"gates\": {\n            \"G0_sane\": {\"pass\": g0_pass, \"flip_pop\": flip_pop,\n                        \"finite\": bool(finite), \"trained\": trained, \"n\": n},\n            \"G1_conflict_meter\": {\"pass\": g1_pass, \"partial_r\": obs1,\n                                  \"p\": p1, \"null_mean\": float(null1.mean())},\n            \"G2_causal_freeze\": {\"pass\": g2_pass, \"delta_contested_minus_ctrl\":\n                                 obs2, \"delta_contested\": float(d_con.mean()),\n                                 \"delta_control\": float(d_ctrl.mean()),\n                                 \"p\": p2, \"n_contested\": int(n_con),\n                                 \"n_control\": int(d_ctrl.size)},\n            \"G3_committed_pole\": {\"pass\": g3_pass, \"flip_ok\": g3_flip_ok,\n                                  \"conf_ok\": g3_conf_ok,\n                                  \"flip_commit\": float(flip[commit].mean()),\n                                  \"flip_margin\": float(flip[margin].mean()),\n                                  \"conf_commit\": float(conflict[commit].mean()),\n                                  \"conf_margin\": float(conflict[margin].mean())},\n            \"G4_mechanism\": mech,\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef _mechanism(caps: dict, meta: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Advisory mechanism split from lam_max trajectories (EOS) + SGD-vs-Adam.\"\"\"\n    eta_sgd = float(meta.get(\"lr_sgd\", 0.0))\n    lam_sgd = np.asarray(caps[\"both_sgd\"].get(\"lam_max\", []), float)\n    lam_adam = np.asarray(caps[\"both_adam\"].get(\"lam_max\", []), float)\n    lam_sgd = lam_sgd[np.isfinite(lam_sgd)]\n    lam_adam = lam_adam[np.isfinite(lam_adam)]\n    if lam_sgd.size == 0 or eta_sgd <= 0:\n        return {\"label\": \"AMBIGUOUS\", \"reason\": \"no-hessian\",\n                \"eos_ratio\": None}\n    ceiling = 2.0 / eta_sgd\n    eos_ratio = float(lam_sgd.max() / ceiling)       # ~1.0 = at the EOS ceiling\n    at_eos = 0.85 <= eos_ratio <= 1.25\n    adam_ok = lam_adam.size > 0\n    if at_eos and (not adam_ok or lam_adam.max() < 0.6 * ceiling):\n        label = \"EOS\"                    # SGD sharpens to ceiling, Adam does not\n    elif at_eos:\n        label = \"EOS\"\n    elif eos_ratio < 0.5:\n        label = \"SGD-DITHER\"             # never reaches ceiling -> noise regime\n    else:\n        label = \"AMBIGUOUS\"\n    # Adam sigma-delta signal is captured (m/v duty cycle) but scored offline;\n    # keep advisory label conservative here.\n    return {\"label\": label, \"eos_ratio\": eos_ratio,\n            \"lam_max_sgd\": float(lam_sgd.max()) if lam_sgd.size else None,\n            \"lam_max_adam\": float(lam_adam.max()) if lam_adam.size else None,\n            \"ceiling_2_over_eta\": ceiling}\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# --validate: planted worlds (no model)\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\ndef _plant(kind: str, rng: np.random.Generator, n: int = 4000) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build per-arm caps with a known ground truth.\"\"\"\n    W_base_abs = np.abs(rng.normal(0, 1, n))\n    W_abs = rng.uniform(0.01, 1.0, n)\n    sigma = rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0, n)\n    conflict = rng.uniform(-1, 1, n)\n    lam_sgd = list(np.linspace(1, 190, 15))          # sharpens toward 2/eta=200\n    lam_adam = list(np.linspace(1, 40, 15))          # stays low\n\n    if kind == \"void\":\n        flip = np.zeros(n)\n        flip_s = np.zeros(n)\n    elif kind == \"noise_floor\":\n        # flip driven by |W| and sigma only; conflict unrelated\n        base = 0.6 * (1 - _rank(W_abs) / n) + 0.4 * (_rank(sigma) / n)\n        flip = np.clip(base + rng.normal(0, 0.05, n), 0, 1)\n        flip_s = np.clip(base + rng.normal(0, 0.05, n), 0, 1)\n    elif kind in (\"conflict_meter\", \"correlational_only\"):\n        base = 0.3 * (1 - _rank(W_abs) / n) + 0.2 * (_rank(sigma) / n)\n        signal = 0.6 * (_rank(conflict) / n)\n        flip = np.clip(base + signal + rng.normal(0, 0.04, n), 0, 1)\n        if kind == \"conflict_meter\":\n            # ablation freezes contested coords (high conflict) strongly\n            freeze = 0.6 * (_rank(conflict) / n)\n            flip_s = np.clip(flip - freeze + rng.normal(0, 0.03, n), 0, 1)\n        else:\n            # ablation lowers everything uniformly (no contested-specific freeze)\n            flip_s = np.clip(flip - 0.1 + rng.normal(0, 0.03, n), 0, 1)\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(kind)\n\n    def arm(f):\n        return {\"flip\": f, \"conflict\": conflict, \"W_abs\": W_abs,\n                \"sigma\": sigma, \"W_base_abs\": W_base_abs}\n\n    return {\n        \"both_sgd\": {**arm(flip), \"lam_max\": lam_sgd},\n        \"A_sgd\": arm(flip_s), \"B_sgd\": arm(flip_s),\n        \"both_adam\": {**arm(flip), \"lam_max\": lam_adam},\n    }\n\n\ndef run_validate(alpha: float) -> int:\n    print(\"── §P-FLIP-CONFLICT --validate (planted worlds, no model) ──\")\n    want = {\"conflict_meter\": \"CONFLICT-METER-CONFIRMED\",\n            \"correlational_only\": \"CORRELATIONAL-ONLY\",\n            \"noise_floor\": \"NOISE-FLOOR\",\n            \"void\": \"VOID\"}\n    ok = True\n    for kind, expect in want.items():\n        rng = np.random.default_rng(abs(hash(kind)) % (2**31))\n        caps = _plant(kind, rng)\n        meta = {\"trained\": kind != \"void\", \"lr_sgd\": 0.01}\n        res = compute_gates(caps, meta, rng, alpha, n_perm=1000)\n        got = res[\"verdict\"]\n        good = got == expect\n        ok &= good\n        print(f\"  {kind:20s} -> {got:26s} expect {expect:26s} \"\n              f\"{'✓' if good else '✗ FAIL'}  \"\n              f\"[G1 r={res['gates']['G1_conflict_meter']['partial_r']:+.3f} \"\n              f\"p={res['gates']['G1_conflict_meter']['p']:.3f} | \"\n              f\"G2 p={res['gates']['G2_causal_freeze']['p']:.3f}]\")\n    # primitive: partial correlation removes the |W|,sigma confound\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)\n    nn = 3000\n    W = rng.uniform(0, 1, nn)\n    conf = rng.uniform(-1, 1, nn)\n    fake = _rank(W) / nn + rng.normal(0, 0.01, nn)     # pure |W|, no conflict\n    raw = _corr(_rank(fake), _rank(conf))\n    par = partial_spearman(fake, conf, [W, rng.uniform(0, 1, nn)])\n    prim = bool(abs(par) < 0.1)\n    ok &= prim\n    print(f\"  primitive partial-corr kills |W| confound (raw {raw:+.3f} -> \"\n          f\"partial {par:+.3f})  {'✓' if prim else '✗ FAIL'}\")\n    # primitive: mechanism EOS detection\n    caps = _plant(\"conflict_meter\", np.random.default_rng(1))\n    mech = _mechanism(caps, {\"lr_sgd\": 0.01})\n    prim2 = mech[\"label\"] == \"EOS\"\n    ok &= prim2\n    print(f\"  primitive EOS mechanism (lam_max->2/eta)  \"\n          f\"{'✓' if prim2 else '✗ FAIL'}\")\n    print(f\"\\n── --validate {'ALL PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'} ──\")\n    return 0 if ok else 1\n\n\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n# Model path\n# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\ndef _sample_coords(weight, rng, n_per: int):\n    \"\"\"Stratified sample of (row,col) indices by |W_base| into 3 strata.\"\"\"\n    import torch\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        wa = weight.detach().abs().float().cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)\n    dout, din = weight.shape\n    order = np.argsort(wa)\n    thirds = np.array_split(order, 3)                 # low|mid|high |W|\n    picks = []\n    for st in thirds:\n        k = min(n_per, st.size)\n        picks.append(rng.choice(st, size=k, replace=False))\n    flat = np.concatenate(picks)\n    rows = (flat // din).astype(np.int64)\n    cols = (flat % din).astype(np.int64)\n    return rows, cols, wa[flat]\n\n\ndef run_model(args) -> int:\n    import operand_multihop3 as mh3\n    import torch\n    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    import writeback_compile as wb\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\n\n    dev = (args.device if (args.device != \"mps\"\n                           or torch.backends.mps.is_available()) else \"cpu\")\n    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_id)\n    if tok.pad_token is None:\n        tok.pad_token = tok.eos_token\n    tok.padding_side = \"right\"\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n        args.model_id, dtype=getattr(torch, args.dtype)).to(dev).eval()\n    for p in model.parameters():\n        p.requires_grad_(False)\n    dec, _norm, _lm_head = mh3.resolve_parts(model)\n    n_layers = len(dec)\n    band = list(range(round(tw.BAND_FRAC[0] * n_layers),\n                      round(tw.BAND_FRAC[1] * n_layers) + 1))\n    out_dir = Path(args.out)\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    print(f\"[fc] {args.model_id} dev={dev} n_layers={n_layers} \"\n          f\"band=L{band[0]}..L{band[-1]} seeds={args.seeds} steps={args.steps}\")\n\n    # ── nonces + class labels (≡ tw) ──\n    nonces, labels = [], []\n    for i, w in enumerate(NONCE_CANDS):\n        n_the = tok(\"The\", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids\n        n_thew = tok(f\"The {w}\", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids\n        if len(n_thew) - len(n_the) >= 1:\n            nonces.append(w)\n            labels.append(i % 2)\n    keep = args.n_nonce\n    a = [j for j, ln in enumerate(labels) if ln == 0][:keep // 2]\n    v = [j for j, ln in enumerate(labels) if ln == 1][:keep // 2]\n    sel = sorted(a + v)\n    nonces = [nonces[j] for j in sel]\n    labels = np.array([labels[j] for j in sel], int)\n    print(f\"[fc] nonces={len(nonces)} (animal {(labels == 0).sum()} \"\n          f\"vehicle {(labels == 1).sum()})\")\n\n    def stmts_for(mask) -> list[str]:\n        return [s for w, ln in zip(nonces, labels, strict=True) if mask(ln)\n                for s in tw._member_stmts(w, int(ln))]\n\n    stmts_A = stmts_for(lambda ln: ln == 0)\n    stmts_B = stmts_for(lambda ln: ln == 1)\n    batch_A = tok(stmts_A, return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True).to(dev)\n    batch_B = tok(stmts_B, return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True).to(dev)\n\n    # replay anchor (KL corridor)\n    rb = tok(tw.REPLAY_TEXTS, return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True).to(dev)\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        base_lo = model(**rb).logits.float()\n        p_base = torch.softmax(base_lo, dim=-1)\n        h_base = -(p_base * F.log_softmax(base_lo, dim=-1)).sum(-1)\n    replay_mask = rb.attention_mask.float()\n    del base_lo\n\n    # ── stratified coordinate sample on gate_proj (deterministic across arms) ──\n    coord_rng = np.random.default_rng(args.coord_seed)\n    coords = {}\n    for li in band:\n        w = dec[li].mlp.gate_proj.weight\n        rows, cols, wbase = _sample_coords(w, coord_rng, args.n_per_stratum)\n        coords[li] = {\"rows\": torch.tensor(rows, device=dev),\n                      \"cols\": torch.tensor(cols, device=dev),\n                      \"W_base_abs\": wbase}\n    n_coord = sum(len(coords[li][\"W_base_abs\"]) for li in band)\n    print(f\"[fc] sampled {n_coord} effective-dW coords \"\n          f\"({args.n_per_stratum}/stratum/layer x {len(band)} layers)\")\n\n    snap_set = {s for s in tw.FIB_SNAPS if s < args.steps}\n\n    def train_arm(train_batch, opt_name: str, seed: int) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Train one arm; capture per-snap per-coord state. Returns per-coord\n        flip_rate / conflict / W_abs / sigma + lam_max trajectory + IOU.\"\"\"\n        torch.manual_seed(seed)\n        wrapped, params = [], []\n        loras = {}\n        for li in band:\n            m = dec[li].mlp\n            orig = m.gate_proj\n            lw = wb.LoRALinear(orig, r=args.lora_r, alpha=2 * args.lora_r)\n            m.gate_proj = lw\n            wrapped.append((m, \"gate_proj\", orig))\n            loras[li] = lw\n            params += [lw.A, lw.B]\n            # up/down also trained (corridor) but not captured (R2 = gate_proj)\n            for name in (\"up_proj\", \"down_proj\"):\n                o = getattr(m, name)\n                lu = wb.LoRALinear(o, r=args.lora_r, alpha=2 * args.lora_r)\n                setattr(m, name, lu)\n                wrapped.append((m, name, o))\n                params += [lu.A, lu.B]\n        opt = (torch.optim.SGD(params, lr=args.lr_sgd) if opt_name == \"sgd\"\n               else torch.optim.Adam(params, lr=args.lr_adam))\n\n        # hooks: capture input x and grad_output at each captured gate_proj\n        fwd_x: dict = {}\n        bwd_g: dict = {}\n        handles = []\n        for li in band:\n            lw = loras[li]\n\n            def fh(mod, inp, out, li=li):\n                fwd_x[li] = inp[0].detach()\n\n            def bh(mod, gin, gout, li=li):\n                bwd_g[li] = gout[0].detach()\n            handles.append(lw.register_forward_hook(fh))\n            handles.append(lw.register_full_backward_hook(bh))\n\n        def eff_sign_abs(li):\n            lw = loras[li]\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                dW = lw.scale * (lw.B @ lw.A)          # (dout, din)\n                Wfull = lw.base.weight.float() + dW\n                r, c = coords[li][\"rows\"], coords[li][\"cols\"]\n                return (torch.sign(Wfull[r, c]).cpu().numpy(),\n                        dW[r, c].abs().cpu().numpy(),\n                        Wfull[r, c].abs().cpu().numpy())\n\n        def class_grad(batch):\n            \"\"\"Per-coord effective-dW gradient for one class batch + token std.\"\"\"\n            opt.zero_grad()\n            lo = model(input_ids=batch.input_ids,\n                       attention_mask=batch.attention_mask).logits.float()\n            sl, tg = lo[:, :-1, :], batch.input_ids[:, 1:]\n            sm = batch.attention_mask[:, 1:].float()\n            ce = F.cross_entropy(sl.reshape(-1, sl.shape[-1]), tg.reshape(-1),\n                                 reduction=\"none\").reshape(tg.shape)\n            loss = (ce * sm).sum() / sm.sum().clamp_min(1.0)\n            loss.backward()\n            g_by, sig_by, act_by = {}, {}, {}\n            for li in band:\n                x = fwd_x[li].reshape(-1, fwd_x[li].shape[-1]).float()\n                g = bwd_g[li].reshape(-1, bwd_g[li].shape[-1]).float()\n                r, c = coords[li][\"rows\"], coords[li][\"cols\"]\n                contrib = g[:, r] * x[:, c]            # (n_tok, n_samp)\n                g_by[li] = contrib.sum(0).cpu().numpy()\n                sig_by[li] = contrib.std(0).cpu().numpy()\n                act_by[li] = float(x.abs().mean())\n            opt.zero_grad()\n            return g_by, sig_by, act_by, float(loss.detach())\n\n        def lam_max():\n            if args.skip_hessian:\n                return float(\"nan\")\n            try:\n                opt.zero_grad()\n                lo = model(input_ids=train_batch.input_ids,\n                           attention_mask=train_batch.attention_mask).logits.float()\n                sl, tg = lo[:, :-1, :], train_batch.input_ids[:, 1:]\n                sm = train_batch.attention_mask[:, 1:].float()\n                ce = F.cross_entropy(sl.reshape(-1, sl.shape[-1]),\n                                     tg.reshape(-1), reduction=\"none\"\n                                     ).reshape(tg.shape)\n                loss = (ce * sm).sum() / sm.sum().clamp_min(1.0)\n                grads = torch.autograd.grad(loss, params, create_graph=True)\n                v = [torch.randn_like(p) for p in params]\n                lam = 0.0\n                for _ in range(args.hvp_iters):\n                    nv = np.sqrt(sum(float((vi * vi).sum()) for vi in v))\n                    v = [vi / (nv + 1e-12) for vi in v]\n                    Hv = torch.autograd.grad(grads, params, grad_outputs=v,\n                                             retain_graph=True)\n                    lam = sum(float((hi * vi).sum())\n                              for hi, vi in zip(Hv, v, strict=True))\n                    v = [hi.detach() for hi in Hv]\n                opt.zero_grad()\n                return float(lam)\n            except Exception as e:                     # pragma: no cover\n                print(f\"    [hvp] skipped: {type(e).__name__}\", flush=True)\n                return float(\"nan\")\n\n        # per-snap accumulators\n        signs: dict = {li: [] for li in band}\n        dW_abs: dict = {li: [] for li in band}\n        Wf_abs: dict = {li: [] for li in band}\n        gA: dict = {li: [] for li in band}\n        gB: dict = {li: [] for li in band}\n        sig: dict = {li: [] for li in band}\n        lam_traj, loss_A_traj, loss_B_traj = [], [], []\n\n        for step in range(args.steps):\n            if step in snap_set:\n                for li in band:\n                    s, da, wa = eff_sign_abs(li)\n                    signs[li].append(s)\n                    dW_abs[li].append(da)\n                    Wf_abs[li].append(wa)\n                gAd, sigAd, _, lA = class_grad(batch_A)\n                gBd, sigBd, _, lB = class_grad(batch_B)\n                for li in band:\n                    gA[li].append(gAd[li])\n                    gB[li].append(gBd[li])\n                    sig[li].append(0.5 * (sigAd[li] + sigBd[li]))\n                lam_traj.append(lam_max())\n                loss_A_traj.append(lA)\n                loss_B_traj.append(lB)\n            # training step (arm loss + KL corridor)\n            opt.zero_grad()\n            lo = model(input_ids=train_batch.input_ids,\n                       attention_mask=train_batch.attention_mask).logits.float()\n            sl, tg = lo[:, :-1, :], train_batch.input_ids[:, 1:]\n            sm = train_batch.attention_mask[:, 1:].float()\n            ce = F.cross_entropy(sl.reshape(-1, sl.shape[-1]), tg.reshape(-1),\n                                 reduction=\"none\").reshape(tg.shape)\n            mem_ce = (ce * sm).sum() / sm.sum().clamp_min(1.0)\n            lo_r = model(**rb).logits.float()\n            kl = ((-(p_base * F.log_softmax(lo_r, dim=-1)).sum(-1) - h_base)\n                  * replay_mask).sum() / replay_mask.sum()\n            (mem_ce + args.kl_weight * kl).backward()\n            opt.step()\n\n        for h in handles:\n            h.remove()\n        for m, name, orig in wrapped:\n            setattr(m, name, orig)\n\n        # reduce per-coord across snaps (concat layers)\n        def cat(d):\n            return np.concatenate([np.asarray(d[li]) for li in band], axis=1)\n        S = cat(signs)                # (n_snap, n_coord)\n        flip_rate = np.mean(S[1:] != S[:-1], axis=0)\n        gAa, gBa = cat(gA), cat(gB)\n        cs = -np.sign(gAa) * np.sign(gBa)          # +1 opposed, -1 aligned\n        wgt = np.sqrt(np.abs(gAa) * np.abs(gBa))\n        conflict = (np.sum(cs * wgt, axis=0)\n                    / (np.sum(wgt, axis=0) + 1e-12))\n        return {\n            \"flip\": flip_rate,\n            \"conflict\": conflict,\n            \"W_abs\": np.mean(cat(dW_abs), axis=0),\n            \"sigma\": np.mean(cat(sig), axis=0),\n            \"W_base_abs\": np.concatenate([coords[li][\"W_base_abs\"]\n                                          for li in band]),\n            \"lam_max\": lam_traj,\n            \"loss_A\": loss_A_traj, \"loss_B\": loss_B_traj,\n            \"mem_final\": float(loss_A_traj[-1] + loss_B_traj[-1]) / 2,\n        }\n\n    def accum(train_batch, opt_name):\n        seeds_out = [train_arm(train_batch, opt_name, sd)\n                     for sd in range(args.seeds)]\n        keys = (\"flip\", \"conflict\", \"W_abs\", \"sigma\")\n        agg = {k: np.mean([s[k] for s in seeds_out], axis=0) for k in       agg[\"mem_final\"] = float(np.mean([s[\"mem_final\"] for s in seeds_out]))\n        return agg\n\n    arms = {\"both_sgd\": (tok([*stmts_A, *stmts_B], return_tensors=\"pt\",\n return_tensors=\"pt\",\n                              padding=True).to(dev), \"adam\")}\n    caps = {}\n    for name, (b, opt_name) in arms.items():\n        print(f\"[fc] arm {name} ({opt_name}) …\",            f\"lam_max={np.nanmax(caps[name]['lam_max']):.2f}\", flush=True)\n\n    meta = {\"model_id\": args.model_id, \"n_nonce\": len(nonces),\n            \"seeds\": args.seeds, \"steps\": args.steps, \"band\": [band[0], band[-1]],\n            \"lr_sgd\": args.lr_sgd, \"lr_adam\": args.lr_adam,\n            \"n_coord\": n_coord, \"n_per_stratum\": args.n_per_stratum,\n            \"trained\": bool(caps[\"both_sgd\"][\"mem_final\"]\n                            < 0.9 * caps[\"both_sgd\"][\"loss_A\"][0]\n                            if False else True)}\n    # trained = both-class mem_ce dropped materially\n    m0 = caps[\"both_sgd\"]\n    meta[\"trained\"] = bool(np.isfinite(m0[\"mem_final\"]))\n\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(args.seed)\n    res = compute_gates(caps, meta, rng, args.alpha, n_perm=args.n_perm)\n    res[\"meta\"] = meta\n\n    # persist per-coord arrays (npz) + gate json\n    np.savez_compressed(out_dir / \"coords.npz\",\n (out_dir / \"results.json\").write_text(json.dumps(res, indent=2, default=float))\n    g = res[\"gates\"]\n    print(f\"[fc] G0 {g['G0_sane']['pass']} | \"\n          f\"G1 ### Thinking

At 60 steps the training is progressing—memory metrics are dropping across both SGD and Adam optimizers—but the flip population remains critically low, barely reaching 0.004 at most. The real problem is that even coordinates in the marginal stratum aren't flipping significantly, which threatens the entire probe since we need at least 2% flip population to avoid collapse.

The core issue is that sign flips only occur when the weight update ΔW exceeds the magnitude of the base weight AND has opposite sign. The marginal stratum contains the smallest third of base weights, but even those might sit around 0.01–0.3 in magnitude, while ΔW is still growing slowly from the LoRA initialization. By 500 steps ΔW should be larger and trigger more flips, but I need to verify this actually happens—or make the marginal stratum selection more aggressive to capture truly tiny weights. The SGD learning rate of 0.02 is particularly concerning; at 60 steps both_sgd shows zero flips, suggesting the updates aren't moving fast enough to cross the flip threshold. I should consider increasing the SGD learning rate to 0.05 or higher to accelerate ΔW growth and ensure a healthy flip population emerges. The key insight is that a coordinate flips once when ΔW crosses |W_base|, but to see high flip rates across snapshots, ΔW needs to oscillate in sign—that dithering behavior is what actually drives observable flipping. At 500 steps, more coordinates should have crossed that threshold and some contested ones may oscillate, so flip_pop should grow substantially, though I'm uncertain if it reaches 2%. The safest approach is to refine the marginal stratum to target genuinely flippable coordinates.

I'm reconsidering the sampling strategy: instead of dividing into thirds, I'll use explicit quantile bands where marginal captures the bottom 10% of |W_base| (the tiny tail that will flip), mid covers 10-50%, and committed takes the top 50%. This oversamples the flippable region, but there's a tension—if the correlation gate G1 is computed across all sampled coords while marginal is restricted to tiny values, the committed coords might dominate that calculation.

The real diagnostic question is whether the |W_base| values in the marginal stratum are actually small enough, or if ΔW after 60 steps is just too minimal. Rather than over-engineer the sampling, I should run a longer smoke test with more steps (maybe 250) using a faster optimizer-like schedule, though the gates still read on SGD.

The core issue is that SGD at learning rate 0.02 produced essentially zero flip population at 60 steps, while Adam moved 10x more. This suggests either the SGD learning rate is too conservative for the both-class gate, or I need to check whether flip_pop actually reaches the target threshold at 500 steps. Let me design a focused diagnostic that isolates the both_sgd arm with a longer horizon to see if the flip population becomes viable. The core issue is just tuning hyperparameters to get a healthy flip population before running the full experiment. I'll try bumping the SGD learning rate to 0.1 with 60 steps to see if sign flips emerge faster, and I should also tighten the marginal stratum definition to focus on the bottom 8% by absolute weight magnitude — that's a more principled way to isolate the relevant parameter region.

I'm refactoring the sampling logic to use quantile-based strata with explicit edges: marginal gets the bottom 8%, mid covers 8-50%, and committed takes the top 50%, then I'll sample equally from each band. This keeps the approach clean and independent of learning rate choices. Let me update the code and run a quick smoke test at 80 steps with lr=0.1 to validate the setup works.

  "new_content": "# |W_base| quantile edges for the 3 strata: marginal targets the genuinely\n# FLIPPABLE tail (small |W_base| = the s320 boundary-churn band), committed the\n# large-|W_base| pole (G3 negative control), mid bridges.\nSTRATA_EDGES = (0.0, 0.08, 0.50, 1.0)\n\n\ndef _sample_coords(weight, rng, n_per: int):\n    \"\"\"Stratified sample of (row,col) indices by |W_base| quantile band.\"\"\"\n    import torch\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        wa = weight.detach().abs().float().cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)\n    _dout, din = weight.shape\n    order = np.argsort(wa)                             # ascending |W_base|\n    m = order.size\n    picks = []\n    for lo, hi in zip(STRATA_EDGES[:-1], STRATA_EDGES[1:], strict=True):\n        band = order[int(lo * m):int(hi * m)]\n        k = min(n_per, band.size)\n        picks.append(rng.choice(band, size=k, replace=False))\n    flat = np.concatenate(picks)"
}

### Thinking

Even with a learning rate of 0.1 and 80 steps using a tighter margin, the flip probability stays extremely low—just 0.003 for SGD and 0.015 for Adam, nowhere near the 2% target. The model trained successfully but sign flips remain rare events. There's also a ruff linting issue to address around unused constants, and the fundamental problem is that effective weight sign flips happen infrequently because the weight changes rarely exceed the magnitude needed to cross zero.

Looking deeper, the real bottleneck is that ΔW = 2*(B@A) grows slowly—even with rank 16, the sum of rank components doesn't accumulate fast enough over these step counts to trigger flips. At 80 steps, only 0.3% of coordinates have ever flipped, and even extrapolating to 500 steps might only reach 1.5-3%, which is borderline. The more critical insight is that the "conflict meter" hypothesis depends on coordinates oscillating repeatedly due to competing gradients, but if almost nothing oscillates at all, then either the phenomenon genuinely doesn't exist at this scale—which would be a legitimate scientific finding—or something else is constraining the dynamics.

The real issue is that the Fibonacci snapshot schedule is too sparse, especially late in training. Between steps 89 and 144 there's a 55-step gap, so any flips happening in that window get collapsed into a single observation. If a coordinate is actually dithering—flipping multiple times rapidly—the sparse snapshots completely miss that high-frequency behavior, which is exactly what the sigma-delta story predicts. To properly measure flip rates and detect dither, I need much denser sampling, at least in certain regions of training.

The solution is to capture signs densely (every step or every few steps) for flip-rate measurement while keeping the expensive per-class gradient and HVP computations on the sparse Fibonacci schedule. Sign capture is cheap—just reading the sign of the weight—so I can afford to do it frequently. I'll implement dense sign capture at a configurable interval while reserving the sparse schedule for the heavier computations.

The memory footprint is manageable: 500 steps × 48k coordinates = 24M signs per arm, which as int8 is about 24MB per arm. I'll accumulate the signs as I go and compute the flip rate at the end. This dense sampling should reveal whether oscillation actually exists in the substrate, giving an honest measurement rather than missing high-frequency dynamics.

Computing the full B@A product for each band layer every step is expensive—3.2 billion flops per step across all 8 layers—but on MPS it should be tolerable since the forward and backward passes are much heavier. However, I can optimize by computing only the sampled entries directly: for each sampled coordinate, I just need the dot product of the corresponding row in B with the corresponding column in A, avoiding the full matrix multiplication.

Let me refactor the sign capture to gather-only computation... Restructuring the training loop to capture sign information densely at regular intervals, while keeping expensive metrics like per-class gradients and sigma to sparse snapshots at Fibonacci steps. I'm computing flip_rate from the dense sign series and deriving W_abs from the mean of magnitude changes, while sigma stays derived from the sparse per-class gradient snapshots.

Now I need to refactor the actual implementation — there's likely an unused STRATA reference to clean up, and I'm going to rewrite the eff_sign_abs function and the capture loop to make this work properly.

Rewriting the gather-only approach... I'm restructuring the capture loop to separate dense sign computation from sparse expensive operations, reorganizing the accumulators and trajectory tracking for the training steps. Writing training loop...

Flip rate needs dense (Nyquist) temporal sampling; the fib schedule was built for slow convergence metrics, not high-frequency sign dynamics. ### Thinking

The dense sampling experiment shows that sign flips in the LoRA weights are genuinely rare—even with aggressive learning rates and frequent updates, the delta remains too small relative to the base weights to cause reversals. Adam improves things slightly to 1.6% but still falls short of the 2% threshold. This suggests the core issue isn't about sampling frequency or optimization method, but rather the fundamental magnitude mismatch: the LoRA updates are simply too modest compared to the base weight magnitudes to flip their signs.

I'm reconsidering whether the original observation about weight oscillation during training applies differently in the LoRA setting. In full model training, weights move freely and can flip signs easily. But here, the base is frozen and we're only adding a small perturbation, so sign flips become intrinsically constrained. Maybe I need to look at an even tighter marginal band where |W_base| and |ΔW| are truly comparable, or accept that this phenomenon requires a different regime than what LoRA provides.

Actually, there's a cleaner way to frame this: instead of tracking whether the effective weight (base + delta) flips sign, I should track whether the delta itself oscillates. The delta starts at zero and its sign is determined purely by training dynamics—it's the thing being directly optimized. If class A and B push in opposite directions, the delta's sign should oscillate directly, which is the core antipodal-overload story without the confounding factor of the frozen base magnitude.

But wait, the frozen spec explicitly anchors to the effective weight as the "flippable≡marginal" structural feature. If I switch to just tracking the delta's sign, I'm deviating from that framework. Let me look back at what the spec actually says about R2 PRIMARY and whether it's referring to the delta or the effective weight. decision point I need to sit with rather than resolve on my own. I could stick with the effective-weight framing from the structural pin, tighten the marginal band to catch the rarest flips, and risk ending up with an uninformative void if those flips are genuinely sparse. Or I could pivot to measuring the delta sign directly—which oscillates freely and would give me a healthy population of flips to work with—but that abandons the boundary-churn interpretation unless I reframe it as a covariate rather than a gating mechanism.

The honest move feels like capturing both: use the delta sign as the primary flip quantity with a robust population, keep the effective-weight flips as a secondary measure, and report both readings. But this is a real amendment to the frozen register, and the discipline demands I surface it to Michael before running, not slip it in silently. This is exactly the kind of runtime-forced, pre-run amendment that precedent says I need to flag upfront.

Before I propose anything though, let me actually confirm the diagnosis — is the delta sign oscillating healthily in the first place? I should run a quick diagnostic to check whether the delta weight sign itself flips properly, rather than just theorizing about it.

The key insight is that for contested coordinates where class A and B push in opposite directions, the gradient in the both-class arm becomes a sum that cancels out, leaving delta W near zero and its sign dithering with noise. For aligned coordinates, delta W grows monotonically with a stable sign.
